      "source": [
        "# Convert long format transactions into a list of lists (basket format)\n",
        "baskets = tx.groupby('order_id')['sku_name'].apply(list).tolist()\n",
        "print(f\"Total customer baskets created: {len(baskets)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# One-hot encode the baskets using mlxtend's TransactionEncoder\n",
        "te = TransactionEncoder()\n",
        "te_ary = te.fit(baskets).transform(baskets)\n",
        "df_encoded = pd.DataFrame(te_ary, columns=te.columns_)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Encoded One-Hot Matrix Shape:\", df_encoded.shape)"

        "from mlxtend.frequent_patterns import association_rules, apriori\n",
        "from mlxtend.preprocessing import TransactionEncoder\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "# Ensure df_encoded exists\n",
        "if 'df_encoded' not in locals() and 'df_encoded' not in globals():\n",
        "    print(\"df_encoded not found in memory. Re-creating one-hot encoded matrix from tx...\")\n",
        "    baskets = tx.groupby('order_id')['sku_name'].apply(list).tolist()\n",
        "    te = TransactionEncoder()\n",
        "    te_ary = te.fit(baskets).transform(baskets)\n",
        "    df_encoded = pd.DataFrame(te_ary, columns=te.columns_)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Ensure frequent_itemsets_apriori exists\n",
        "if 'frequent_itemsets_apriori' not in locals() and 'frequent_itemsets_apriori' not in globals():\n",
        "    print(\"frequent_itemsets_apriori not found in memory. Re-running apriori...\")\n",
        "    frequent_itemsets_apriori = apriori(df_encoded, min_support=0.02, use_colnames=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Generate association rules with a minimum lift threshold of 1.0\n",
        "rules = association_rules(frequent_itemsets_apriori, metric='lift', min_threshold=1.0)\n",
        "rules = rules.sort_values('lift', ascending=False)\n",
        "print(f\"Total rules generated: {len(rules)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Filter for ACTIONABLE rules (Support > 5% and Lift > 2.0)\n",
        "actionable_rules = rules[(rules['support'] > 0.05) & (rules['lift'] > 2.0)]\n",
        "print(f\"Actionable rules count: {len(actionable_rules)}\")\n",
        "print(actionable_rules[['antecedents', 'consequents', 'support', 'confidence', 'lift']].head(5))"
